<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

  </div>

<script>
/*
  Small touch-friendly JS to toggle the hover/alternate image on touch devices.
  - Adds 'image-flipped' class on first tap (so user sees alternate view).
  - On second tap it allows the link click (so they can navigate).
  - Non-intrusive: only activates on touch-enabled devices.
*/
(function(){
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) return;

  // use event delegation in case cards are rendered dynamically
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.product-card');
    if (!card) return;

    // if already flipped, allow the link to proceed (do nothing)
    if (card.classList.contains('image-flipped')) {
      // remove flip after a short delay so repeated taps behave naturally
      setTimeout(function(){ card.classList.remove('image-flipped'); }, 600);
      return; // allow the anchor to handle navigation
    }

    // Prevent the link on the first tap and flip image instead
    var anchor = card.querySelector('.product-card-link');
    if (anchor && (e.target === anchor || anchor.contains(e.target))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      card.classList.add('image-flipped');

      // remove flipped state if user taps anywhere else on the document
      var removeFn = function(ev){
        if (!card.contains(ev.target)) {
          card.classList.remove('image-flipped');
          document.removeEventListener('click', removeFn);
        }
      };
      document.addEventListener('click', removeFn);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
})();
</script>

          </div>
        
      </div>

      <!-- Empty State -->
      <div class="collection-empty" id="collection-empty" style="display: none;">

          <path d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z"></path>
        </svg>
        <h3>No products found</h3>
        <p>Try adjusting your filters to see more products.</p>
        <button class="clear-filters-btn" onclick="clearAllFilters()">Clear Filters</button>
      </div>
